    OVERVIEW                                                             概覽

    China East Education Holdings Limited (the “Company”) and its        中國東方教育控股有限公司（「本公司」）及
    subsidiaries (collectively, the “Group”) are a leading provider in   其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）為中國領先職
    the vocational training education in China. Our business focuses     業技能教育提供商。我們的業務集中在為
    on providing innovative vocational training education to students    學生提供創新性職業技能教育，從而提高
    to increase their employability in the ever-changing employment      學生在不斷變化的就業市場中的就業能
    market, as well as supporting China’s evolving workforce             力，並支持因中國持續的經濟增長，城鎮
    requirements driven by its sustained economic growth, urbanization   化及產業升級而帶來的不斷變化的勞動力
    and industrial upgrade.                                              需求。


    Our vocational training education mainly covers five industry        我們的職業技能教育主要涵蓋五個行業分
    segments, namely, culinary arts, western cuisine and pastry,         部，即烹飪技術、西點西餐、信息技術及
    information technology and internet technology, auto services as     互聯網技術、汽車服務以及時尚美業。我
    well as fashion and beauty. We serve these five industry segments    們於七個學校品牌（即新東方烹飪教育、美
    under seven school brands, namely New East Culinary Education,       味學院、歐米奇西點西餐教育、新華電腦
    Cuisine Academy, Omick Education of Western Cuisine and Pastry,      教育、華信智原DT人才培訓基地、萬通汽
    Xinhua Internet Technology Education, Wisezone Data Technology       車教育及歐曼諦時尚美業教育）旗下服務於
    Education, Wontone Automotive Education and On-mind Fashion          該五個行業分部。截至2026年6月30日，我
    and Beauty Education. As of 30 June 2026, we operated 233            們運營233所學校及中心，截至2026年6月
    schools and centers with 158,039 average number of students          30日止六個月的平均培訓人次及客戶註冊
    enrolled and customers registered for the six months ended 30        人數為158,039人次。我們全面且全國性學
    June 2026. Our comprehensive nationwide school network covers        校網絡遍佈中國內地大部份省份及香港。
    almost all of the provinces in mainland China and Hong Kong.

well as auto services. Headquartered in Hefei, Anhui province, we      中國領先地位。我們的總部設在安徽省合
have established a nationwide school network consisting of 233         肥市，校園網絡遍佈全國，截至2026年6月
schools and centers in operation as of 30 June 2026, spanning          30日，我們已在中國內地幾乎所有省份及
almost all of the provinces in mainland China and Hong Kong.           香港運營233所學校及中心。我們以七大學
We operate our business and establish our schools and centers          校品牌（即新東方烹飪教育、美味學院、歐
under seven school brands, namely, New East Culinary Education,        米奇西點西餐教育、新華電腦教育、華信

and Beauty Education.


We focus on providing vocational training education in China in        我們專注於在中國提供烹飪技術、西點西
culinary arts, western cuisine and pastry, information technology      餐、信息技術及互聯網技術、汽車服務以

beauty. These industry sectors are areas in China where there is       業領域為中國對職業技能教育存在龐大需
significant unmet demand for vocational training education to bridge   求但需求尚未得到滿足的領域，旨在彌合
the supply and demand gap between employers and students.              僱主與學生之間的供需缺口。我們的主要

employability and their average compensation levels.

     CULINARY ARTS
     烹飪技術

     New East Culinary Education             76      New East Culinary Education has been providing comprehensive
     (“New East”)                                    culinary training programs to students who pursue a career in
                                                     becoming professional chefs. We teach our students the cooking
                                                     traditions and practices of diversified Chinese cuisines, including the
                                                     well-known and widely recognized eight regional cuisines in China,
                                                     supported by an integration of classic Chinese and Western culinary
                                                     skills. Each of our schools under New East Culinary Education offers
                                                     various culinary training programs with different program lengths to
                                                     meet students’ differentiated learning focuses and demands.
     新東方烹飪教育（
            「新東方」
                ）                            76      新東方烹飪教育一直為追求成為專業廚師的學生提供全面烹飪培訓
                                                     課程。我們教授學生烹飪中國傳統菜餚及練習多種菜餚（包括中國知
                                                     名及受廣泛認可的八大地方菜系）以及傳授中西方經典烹飪技能。
                                                     新東方烹飪教育旗下各所學校提供不同課程時長的各種烹飪培訓課
                                                     程，以滿足學生不同的學習重點和需求。

     Cuisine Academy                         16      Cuisine Academy has been providing customized catering experience
                                                     services to customers who are interested in cooking or who plan
                                                     to establish their own businesses in the catering industry. Cuisine
                                                     Academy primarily provides customers with customized catering
                                                     experience programs.
     美味學院                                    16      美味學院為對烹飪感興趣或計劃於餐飲行業建立自己的公司的客戶
                                                     提供私人訂製烹飪體驗服務。美味學院主要為客戶提供私人訂製烹
                                                     飪體驗課程。

     Cuisine and Pastry (“Omick”)                    western style catering education, which is committed to providing
                                                     specialized culinary training to students with a focus on western
                                                     pastry and western food. We offer a variety of courses, including
                                                     baking, desserts, western cuisines, bartending, and barista training.

Investment Strategy and Future Prospects                                         投資策略及未來展望

The Group’s investments in other financial assets have been                      本集團於其他金融資產的投資乃以有關投
conducted on the premise that such investments would not affect                  資將不會影響業務營運或資本開支為前提，
our business operation or capital expenditures so as to generate a               從而自有關投資獲得較固定銀行存款相對
relatively higher return from such investments than fixed-term bank              較高的回報。
deposits.

The Group has implemented a set of internal control and risk                     本集團已實施一套內部控制及風險管理措
management measures to manage our risks related to investments                   施，以管理投資其他金融資產相關風險。
in other financial assets.

Regarding the investments in financial assets measured at FVTPL                  就有關投資於以公允價值計量並計入損益
and structured deposits, the measures include, among other                       之金融資產及結構性存款的該等措施包括
things, the followings:                                                          （其中包括）以下各項：

•        we analyze such financial assets regularly and keep track of            •      我們定期分析這些金融資產並記錄其

In 2020, the Group became one of the first batch of third-party             於2020年，本集團成為中國首批職業技
rating organisations for the recognition of vocational skills in China,     能等級認定第三方評價機構之一，允許本

the recognition of the skill level of labors by the rating organisation     源和社會保障部（「人社部」）備案的評價機
filed by the Ministry of Human Resources and Social Security                構，對勞動者技能水平進行認定的行為，
(“Ministry of Human Resources and Social Security”), which is               是一種與職業資格評價並行的技能人才評
a way for rating of the skills of talents implemented together with the     價方式。國家職業技能等級認定第三方評
occupational qualification rating. The third-party organisations for        價機構是人社部認可的、在行業內有著
the recognition of vocational skills in China are the unit organisations    領先地位和公信力的單位機構。經自主申
recognised by the Ministry of Human Resources and Social Security,          報、專家遴選、信用核查、註冊地人力資
which are unit organisations with leading positions in the industry         源和社會保障部門實地考核及徵求社會
with credibility. After procedures such as self-reporting, selection by     各方面意見等程序，本集團成為國家首批
experts, credit checks, assessment by the local human resources             職業技能等級認定第三方評價機構之一，

Remuneration Policies                                                  薪酬政策


The remuneration packages of the employees of the Group are            本集團僱員的薪酬待遇乃根據個人資質、
determined with reference to individual qualification, experience,     經驗、表現、對本集團的貢獻及現行市場
performance, contribution to the Group and prevailing market rate.     薪酬水平而釐定。


We remunerate our employees with basic salaries as well                我們向僱員支付基本工資和績效獎金，並
as performance-based bonuses. We determine employee                    基於僱員表現及資歷釐定僱員薪酬。我們
compensation based on each employee’s performance and                  打算在擴展業務時僱用更多教師、導師和
qualifications. We plan to hire additional teachers, instructors and   其他員工。我們招聘僱員渠道包括口碑推
other employees as we expand. Our employee recruiting channels         薦、校園招聘和網絡招聘。
include word-of-mouth referrals, on-campus recruiting and online
recruiting.


Our full-time employees in China participate in a variety of social    我們中國的全職僱員參加中國當地政府管
security plans that are administered by the PRC local governments,     理的各種社會保障計劃，包括但不限於養
including but not limited to, pension benefits, medical care,          老金福利、醫保、失業保險、生育保險、
unemployment insurance, maternity insurance, work injury               工傷保險及住房公積金。按照中國勞工組
insurance and housing provident funds. Chinese labor regulations       織規定，我們的中國附屬公司按僱員上一
require that our PRC subsidiaries make contributions to the            年度的平均薪酬的固定百分比就該等福利
government for these benefits based on a fixed percentage of the       向政府作出供款。
employees’ average salaries of last year.

Our full-time employees in Hong Kong participate in a Mandatory        香港全職僱員參與強制性公積金計劃（「強
Provident Fund Scheme (the “MPF Scheme”) which the assets of           積金計劃」），強積金計劃之資產與本集團
the MPF Scheme are held separately from those of the Group in          資金分開持有及受獨立信託人管理。根據
funds under the control of independent trustees. Under the MPF         強積金計劃，供款金額按參與強積金計劃
Scheme, contributions are made based on a percentage of the            之僱員於本集團之相關收入之某一百分比
participating employees’ relevant income from the Group and the        作出，本集團就強積金計劃唯一承擔之責
only obligation of the Group with respect to the MPF Scheme is to      任為根據該計劃作出所需供款。
make the required contributions under the MPF Scheme.

     CORPORATE GOVERNANCE CODE PRACTICES                                         企業管治常規

     The Company has adopted the principles of the code provisions set           本公司已採納上市規則附錄 C1 所載《企業
     out in the Corporate Governance Code as set out in Appendix C1 to           管治守則》
                                                                                     （「企業管治守則」
                                                                                             ）所載守則條文
     the Listing Rules (the “CG Code”). During the six months ended 30           原則。於截至 2026 年 6 月 30 日止六個月期
     June 2026, the Company has complied with all the code provisions            間，本公司已遵守企業管治守則所載之所
     set out in the CG Code.                                                     有守則條文。


     The Board believes that good corporate governance is essential              董事會相信，為發展本集團及維護股東、
     to the development of the Group and to safeguard the interests              潛在投資者及業務夥伴的利益，維持良好

    Under the EIT Law of the PRC, withholding tax is imposed              根據中國企業所得稅法，由2008年
    on dividends declared in respect of profits earned by PRC             1月1日起，就中國附屬公司賺取的
    subsidiaries from 1 January 2008 onwards. For investors               利潤所宣派的股息須繳納預扣稅。
    incorporated in Hong Kong, a preferential rate of 5%                  在香港註冊成立的投資者將採用5%
    will be applied where appropriate. Other than the PRC                 的優惠稅率（倘適用）。除上述就中
    withholding income tax provided in respect of undistributed           國附屬公司未分派溢利計提中國預
    profits of the PRC subsidiaries as above, no deferred tax             扣所得稅撥備外，並無就自2008年1
    liabilities has been provided for the remaining profits of            月1日起來自中國附屬公司約人民幣
    approximately RMB4,027,443,000 (31 December 2025:                     4,027,443,000元（於2025年12月31
    RMB3,613,835,000), which was derived from the PRC                     日：人民幣3,613,835,000元）的剩餘
    subsidiaries since 1 January 2008 as the Group is able to             溢利計提任何遞延稅項負債，原因是
    control the timing of the reversal of the temporary differences       本集團可控制暫時性差額撥回之時
    and it is probable that the temporary differences will not            間，且該等暫時性差額於可見將來將
    reverse in the foreseeable future.                                    不會撥回。

           The fair value of the Pre-IPO Share Option Scheme was               首次公開發售前購股權計劃的公允
           determined at the date of grant using the Black-scholes             價值乃於授出日期採用柏力克–舒爾
           option pricing model (the “Black-scholes model”).                   斯期權定價模式（「柏力克–舒爾斯模
                                                                               式」
                                                                                ）釐定。


           The Black-scholes model has been used to estimate the fair          柏力克–舒爾斯模式用來估計期權的
           value of the options. The variables and assumptions used in         公允價值。計算購股權公允價值時所
           computing the fair value of the share options are based on the      用的變量及假設乃基於董事的最佳估
           directors’ best estimate. Expected volatility was determined        計。預期波幅使用可比公司股份價格
           by using the historical volatility of comparable company’s          的歷史波幅折減適銷性確定。變量及
           share prices with discounts for lack of marketability. Changes      假設不同，得出的期權公允價值或會
           in variables and assumptions may result in changes in the fair      不同。
           value of the options.

     20. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS OF FINANCIAL                                 20. 金融工具公允價值計量
         INSTRUMENTS

           Fair value of the Group’s financial assets that are measured         按經常性基準以公允價值計量之本集
           at fair value on a recurring basis                                   團金融資產之公允價值


           Some of the Group’s financial assets are measured at fair            本集團部分金融資產於各報告期末按
           value at the end of each reporting period. The following             公允價值計量。下表提供此等金融
           table gives information about how the fair values of these           資產公允價值釐定方法（特別是所用
           financial assets are determined (in particular, the valuation        估值技術及輸入數據）之資料，以及
           technique(s) and inputs used), as well as the level of the fair      根據公允價值計量之輸入數據的可觀
           value hierarchy into which the fair value measurements are           察程度而將公允價值計量分類歸入公
           categorised (Levels 1 to 3) based on the degree to which the         允價值等級之層級（第一至三級）之資
           inputs to the fair value measurements is observable.                 料。


           •     Level 1 fair value measurements are based on quoted            •    第一級公允價值計量指根據於
                 prices (unadjusted) in active market for identical                  計量日期實體可以取得的相同
                 assets or liabilities that the entity can access at the             資產或負債於活躍市場之報價
                 measurement date;                                                   （未經調整）所進行之計量；


           •     Level 2 fair value measurements are those derived from         •    第二級公允價值計量指以第一
                 inputs other than quoted prices included within Level               級報價以外之資產或負債之可
                 1 that are observable for the asset or liability, either            觀察輸入數據，無論是直接（即
                 directly (i.e. as prices) or indirectly (i.e. derived from          價格）或間接（即按價格推算）所
                 prices); and                                                        進行之計量；及


           •     Level 3 fair value measurements are those derived from         •    第三級公允價值計量指透過運
                 valuation techniques that include the lowest level inputs           用並非基於可觀察市場數據之
                 which are significant to the fair value measurement for             對公允值計量屬重大之最低水
                 the asset or liability that are not based on observable             平資產或負債輸入數據（重大不
                 market data (significant unobservable inputs).                      可觀察輸入數據）之估值技術所
                                                                                     進行之計量。
